 3231 | 12               PGT.TCPTEST.CODE.S      2 | Comment     | 00 |         |   -1 | 00/FFFF               | * PGTTCPTest active-open phase
 3232 | 12               PGT.TCPTEST.CODE.S      3 | Comment     | 00 |         |   -1 | 00/FFFF               | *
 3233 | 12               PGT.TCPTEST.CODE.S      4 | Comment     | 00 |         |   -1 | 00/FFFF               | * This is intentionally a wire/state diagnostic rather than the streaming
 3234 | 12               PGT.TCPTEST.CODE.S      5 | Comment     | 00 |         |   -1 | 00/FFFF               | * hot path.  It obtains a DHCP lease, resolves the TCP server's next-hop MAC,
 3235 | 12               PGT.TCPTEST.CODE.S      6 | Comment     | 00 |         |   -1 | 00/FFFF               | * sends a checked SYN, accepts only the matching checksum-valid SYN+ACK, and
 3236 | 12               PGT.TCPTEST.CODE.S      7 | Comment     | 00 |         |   -1 | 00/FFFF               | * sends the final ACK followed by FIN+ACK.  The next gate replaces RXFrame
 3237 | 12               PGT.TCPTEST.CODE.S      8 | Comment     | 00 |         |   -1 | 00/FFFF               | * staging with the transactional W5100-to-DOC ring copy.
 3238 | 12               PGT.TCPTEST.CODE.S      9 | Comment     | 00 |         |   -1 | 00/FFFF               | *
